Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The post-quantum variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 623 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 955 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the post-quantum space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 623 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 955. QED.
